| --- | --- |
| **PS-1** | **What is a wave's unit of work** --- one impulse, one frontier, one host, or a time budget? Determines what a wave's footprint declaration looks like |
| **PS-2** | **Who claims?** `Meta::Schedule`'s `claimed` status has no producer. Is the claimer a worker, an opportunistic rider on the next request touching that host, or both? |
| **PS-3** | **What is the settle-state vocabulary**, and is `unsettled` a value in the lattice or a flag beside it? Four consumers now want it; none has specified it |
| **PS-4** | **Is heat one artifact or two?** The scheduling heuristic may be cheap and approximate; the context-relevance signal AB-22/AB-24/C3 want may not be. **Answer before building either** |
| **PS-5** | **Glitch policy** --- topologically ordered worklist, or accept transient wrong values? Coyote will not force the answer, which is why it needs deciding rather than discovering |
| **PS-6** | **Cycle guard** --- iteration cap, damping, or a declared acyclicity constraint per relation kind? |
| **PS-7** | **Does narration ride the same wave as state, or a separate one?** They decay differently (transient vs persistent) and the narration must be delivered in an order a reader can follow, which is [relational narration](../../AGENT.relationalNarration.planning.md)'s standing problem |
| **PS-8** | **What does the unbiasedness result require of us?** Path tracing's Russian roulette is exact in expectation *because survivors are rescaled*. Whether an analogous correction is meaningful for discrete state --- or whether *parked, never dead* makes it moot --- is unexamined. **Amended 2026-08-07:** the *makes it moot* half now leans on a premise that is itself provisional (PS-9), so this row cannot be discharged by appeal to parking |
| **PS-9** | **What bounds the parked set?** Opened 2026-08-07 from the objection that *never dead* is an unbounded history whose worst case lands exactly on a read. **Leading candidate is collapse-not-queue** --- parked work as a **pending delta per node** rather than a log of impulses, bounding it by graph size rather than event count, which [the dataflow reframe](#retraction-dissolves-under-a-dataflow-reading-and-that-reading-is-the-ludic-native-one) arguably already implies. **What must be checked:** whether every relation kind's impulses actually *are* collapsible (order-independent and idempotent on the target), because a kind whose impulses do not commute cannot be collapsed and would keep its queue |
| **PS-10** | **How does a lead die on purpose?** Opened 2026-08-07. The world needs to be able to say *this happened, it might have mattered, nobody cared, stop pushing* --- and the scheme has only indefinite deferral. **Separate the two properties the original argument conflated:** termination must be **auditable** (that is all the trap-door complaint required); it need not be **revivable**. Candidates: expiry past a horizon into an auditable dead record; **rederive-on-read** for any node whose state has surviving sources, so parking is mandatory only for facts with no cause record; or letting sustained absence of heat be terminal rather than merely deferring. **Note this is a fiction requirement before it is a scalability one** --- a world that never drops a thread can never close one |
| **PS-11** | **Is *never dead* only a persistent-state rule?** Opened 2026-08-07. [The narration/state split](#the-distinction-the-scheme-requires-narration-versus-state) says decay is free for transient/narration impulses and lossy only for persistent state. If that is right, *never dead* was over-generalised from the one row that needed it, and PS-9/PS-10 shrink to the state column. **Cheap to settle and it gates the other two, so settle it first** |

### What this does for the name

**A graph is static structure; a network is a medium things propagate through.** If causality works by impulses settling and heat flowing back the other way, ***Ludic Network* stops being a nicer word for the ensemble and starts describing what the ensemble does** --- a second, independent justification for the term recorded under [the naming consequence](#the-naming-consequence-positiongraph-will-be-lying), and a stronger one than kind-neutrality alone.

---

## Proposal P5: improvisational licence as a first-class graph item

**Recorded 2026-08-09 from conversation. Not promoted to a working hypothesis** --- H3 is in flight, and [candidate H2](#candidate-h2-not-adopted-ab-12s-privileged-level-reframe)'s reason for one-at-a-time still holds.

**Written as a section rather than a row on this plan's own stated criterion** --- [P2](#proposal-p2-separate-the-storage-shard-from-the-containment-relation) is a section "because it touches AB-2, AB-3, AB-4, AB-9, AB-16, AB-30 and AB-32." P5 touches **AB-9, AB-14, AB-19, AB-29, AB-42, AB-43, AB-44 and AB-45**, reorganizes all four of the negatives-cluster rows, and reaches [C17](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c17-the-professional-kitchen-the-chefs-knife-and-the-stand-mixer-nobody-asked-about) and [C18](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c18-three-place-settings-and-only-two-of-them-may-have-a-napkin). Those rows stay open and are **restated in licence terms** rather than absorbed.

### The scheme, as a clause table (numbered 2026-08-09)

**Improvisational licence is a first-class item in the graph, attached to an entity, and manipulable like anything else in it.**

**Numbered on [H3](#h3-ports-as-the-boundary-mechanism-decomposition-as-a-modellers-choice)'s precedent, and *before* the first falsification case rather than after** --- H3's nine clauses exist so a failure lands somewhere specific and rolls back per clause, and P5 was three clauses of prose carrying a great many consequences. **The exercise paid for itself immediately: numbering surfaced four claims P5 was already relying on without stating** --- clauses 9, 10 and 11, plus a refinement to 7 that its source case had conflated. **An unstated claim cannot be falsified, so those four were unfalsifiable until now**, which is the argument for doing this first.

| # | Clause | Status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **1** | **Licence is unbounded by default.** An entity with no recorded limit permits any improvisation within its scope | **Established.** [C17](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c17-the-professional-kitchen-the-chefs-knife-and-the-stand-mixer-nobody-asked-about)'s three-value model stated from the other side --- *not established* costs nothing, which is what lets an infinite detail space go unrepresented. **This is now the clause C20 attacks** --- re-aimed here on 2026-08-09 from clause 3, which turned out to be a wording problem rather than a contradiction. The tightly-authored closed world, where an author wants silence to mean **no**, is the falsification target, and it is **independent of relevance**, which is why it replaced the tavern version |
| **2** | **A negative is a recorded *narrowing* of licence, not a fact asserted about the world.** *There is no deep fryer* is *the kitchen's licence, closed over deep-fryer-shaped things*; a blanket negative is a narrowing to zero | **Established.** The inversion [C18](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c18-three-place-settings-and-only-two-of-them-may-have-a-napkin) Finding 5 recorded, and what dissolved AB-43's blanket half. Also what keeps C17's closed-world defect from returning: a narrowing is not a completeness claim |
| **3** | **Scope is the entity the licence attaches to, and an entity's scope includes what it contains** --- so a limit reaches **inward** freely and never **outward** | **Established, after a correction on 2026-08-09.** Three independent arrivals support the no-outward half (C18's hungry absence, C19's mis-scoped genre rule, the fault section). ~~**Under direct attack in the other direction** --- a tavern authored *poor* must reach inward, and this clause forbids it; the clause is required to both not-reach and reach, C11's internal-contradiction mode.~~ **Withdrawn: the contradiction was an artifact of the original wording**, which said *no further* without saying **in which direction**. A place setting's limit reaching the tavern is **outside** its holder-scope and forbidden; the tavern's limit reaching a place setting **inside** it is **within** its holder-scope and permitted. **Containment scope is directional by nature**, and reading *no further* as direction-neutral was the error. **Note what the clause does and does not do now:** it says a container's limits *may* reach inward; **which** of them do is [PL-1](#open-questions-p5-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused)'s question, not this clause's. The clause permits; relevance selects |
| **4** | **Licence is a first-class graph item, attached to an entity and manipulable** | **Open --- this is AB-42.** C19 shows a licence that *changes*, which supports *manipulable* but says nothing about node-versus-collection |
| **5** | **Licence import is a whole acquiring an intensional description** --- no mechanism of its own | **Established, on one case.** [C19](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c19-filling-out-the-place-setting-and-the-tavern-that-ran-out-of-napkins) beat 3. Notable for falling out of C18's existing discriminator rather than needing new machinery, which is this plan's weak evidence-of-a-real-joint signal --- but one case is one case |
| **6** | **Import is a copy, not a reference; licence provenance is not traced and licence changes do not cascade** | **Settled as *policy*, with a re-open trigger --- a different category from the shape clauses and it must not be swept in with them.** See [Licence transfer](#licence-transfer-import-is-a-copy-and-provenance-is-deliberately-not-traced-2026-08-09). Defended on cost *and* on the local answer being the fictionally correct one |
| **7** | **Whether a whole has an *intensional* description or is merely *extensional* determines whether it has a licence at all** | **Established by C18 --- and corrected here.** C18 states the discriminator as *intensional -> open, extensional -> closed*. **That conflates two things.** A *poor* tavern is intensional and **narrow**, so intensionality determines whether a licence **exists and what shape it has**, not whether it is open. **Found by numbering, before C20 was written** |
| **8** | **Causality propagates; licence does not.** Permission is held, not transmitted --- register propagation policy *none* | **Established on one case (C19), and a deliberate asymmetry with [P4](#proposal-p4-settling-dataflow-and-narrative-heat) rather than an oversight.** Note clause 3's attack does not touch this: *reaching inward by scope* is not the same as *propagating along edges* |
| **9** | **A licence is a *shaped space*, not a scalar** --- an entity may be open in some dimensions and closed in others | **Open, and unstated in P5 until now.** Surfaced by C20's *warm but poor* tavern: warmth and poverty are simultaneous and pull opposite ways, so a single openness dial cannot express it. **Puts real weight on AB-43's unsolved half** (describing a closed region) and on AB-42 (what a licence record is) |
| **10** | ~~**Entities not related by import have no licence relationship.**~~ **Restated 2026-08-09: permission is *copied* at import; context is *read live* via relevance** | ~~Open, the gap C20 targets second.~~ **Substantially resolved rather than falsified, and the resolution is the useful kind --- it names a distinction that was missing rather than removing one.** The original claim was a consequence of clause 3 read direction-neutrally; once clause 3 permits inward reach, a never-imported entity **does** have a relationship to its container --- it reads ancestor facts live, selected by [PL-1](#open-questions-p5-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused). **So *imported-from* and *merely-inside* differ as snapshot versus live read**, which also stops clause 6's no-cascade from conflicting with live relevance: **the thing that does not cascade is *permission*; the thing read live is *context*.** Residual work belongs to PL-1, not here |
| **11** | **Authoring sets licence unattenuated; improvisation operates only within licence** | **Open --- proposed in discussion, never worked.** Authoring is not delegation, so it is outside any attenuation scheme; this is also the third route by which the asset-added branch of AB-29 arrives. C20 has an *authorially* constrained tavern, so it exercises this clause directly |

**Tagging convention, on H3's terms:** mark a row `P5-dependent` only when it is **derived from** a clause, not merely compatible with one --- and **cite the clause number**, since the whole point of numbering is that clause 6 falling should not revert rows that only ever needed clause 2.

### What the reframe buys, stated as four specific repairs

| Problem, as previously recorded | Under P5 |
| --- | --- |
| **The completeness trap** ([C18](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c18-three-place-settings-and-only-two-of-them-may-have-a-napkin) Finding 4) --- *no napkin here* must not be read as *no napkins in the tavern* | **Becomes unrepresentable rather than forbidden.** The limit attaches to the place setting, so it cannot reach the tavern. **A rule became an invariant**, which is the stronger form and the reason to prefer this framing over the warning it replaces |
| **AB-43** --- what is a negative keyed by, when its referent does not exist? | **Dissolved for the blanket case** (keyed by the licensing entity, an id that exists) and **reshaped for the narrowing case**: no longer *give identity to a nonentity*, which is ill-posed, but *describe a closed region of licence space*, which is well-posed and comparably hard |
| **AB-42's accumulation cost** --- negatives are cheaper to create than positives and accrue faster | **Collapses from N nodes to one licence per entity whose content narrows.** And a licence **coarsens** (*this place setting is closed*) where independent negative nodes do not --- which matters precisely because the cost was about LLM context |
| **AB-45's provenance collision** --- licence is provenance-determined, but AB-14 holds provenance is invisible downstream | **Resolved.** Licence is carried *by the whole* as a first-class property; provenance only sets its initial value. Nothing downstream introspects how the whole came to exist, so the merge-layer claim survives intact |

**One consistency check, run deliberately because a unification that quietly costs a settled property is the failure mode:** *not established* still costs nothing. Licence is recorded only when **narrowed**, so C17's three-value model is preserved rather than traded away.

### What it does not resolve

- **AB-43 for narrowing licences** --- describing a closed region is still unanswered.
- **Whether licence-closure and established-absence are the same fact.** *There is no battery* must be a **world** fact for the deduction *therefore it is dark* to run; *this place setting is closed* is only a permission. They plausibly coincide, since the only route to a battery appearing there is improvisation --- **except through authoring**, which is not delegated and can add a deep fryer to a kitchen whose licence is closed. **That exception is [AB-29](#open-decisions-design--plan-only)'s asset-added branch arriving from a third direction.** AB-44 therefore survives in narrower form: **every established negative closes licence; only some also carry causal force.**
- **Composition of licences** --- how nested and layered licences combine, and how positives and negatives merge under manipulation. **Open; the leading frame is capability attenuation** (a delegated authority may only narrow), with **authoring explicitly outside it**. **Subsumed as the licence-shaped half of [PL-1](#open-questions-p5-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused)**, which asks the same question over positive facts as well.
- **Divergence** --- see the cost below.

### Licence transfer: import is a copy, and provenance is deliberately not traced (2026-08-09)

**The operation, from [C19](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c19-filling-out-the-place-setting-and-the-tavern-that-ran-out-of-napkins).** A recognized place setting is licence-zero. The player says *"fill out the place setting with everything else it needs."* Licence is **imported** from the tavern --- which has an open licence to improvise tavern furnishings --- into the place setting, whose limit is removed. **It thereafter holds its own, independent licence.**

**What the import actually is, and it is not a new mechanism.** [C18](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c18-three-place-settings-and-only-two-of-them-may-have-a-napkin)'s discriminator is **intensional versus extensional**: a recognized whole is extensional --- its member set *is* its content --- which is exactly why its licence is zero. The player's utterance supplies an **intensional description** (*everything a place setting needs*). **Licence import is a whole acquiring an intensional description.** **So C18's three cases are three *states*, not three kinds, and there is an operation between them** --- which C18 did not say and, read alone, implies the opposite. **The negative lock is not permanent**, and nothing in the model made it so.

**Import is a copy, not a reference.** Once imported, the licence is the place setting's own. A later tavern-level narrowing --- *the tavern has no napkins in stock* --- **does not reach into it**, and the place setting may still improvise a napkin.

**Explicitly declined, and recorded as a decision rather than an omission: licence provenance is not traced and licence changes do not cascade.** It is *possible* to keep the provenance graph of every imported licence and propagate narrowings through it. **It is not worth it:** an immense standing cost --- every licence read potentially walking a history, every narrowing potentially cascading --- to correct a small set of edge cases. **This plan is choosing not to be constrained by it.**

**And the local reading is usually the *fictionally correct* one, which upgrades this from a compromise to a preference.** The napkin was laid out before anyone found the storeroom empty; a tavern being out of napkins **now** is a claim about stock, not about what is already set on a table. **The cheaper answer is also the better one in the motivating case**, which is worth stating so this is not remembered as a scalability concession.

**The one case where it does bite, and the repair is scope rather than propagation.** If *no napkins* is meant as a **world or genre rule** rather than a stock fact, a place setting improvising one is genuinely wrong. **The fix is the move that killed the scope trap, arriving a third time:** attach the limit at the scope it actually holds at. A genre rule is not the tavern's licence and should never have been recorded as one. **Propagation is the wrong repair for a mis-scoped limit.**

**The principle this establishes, stated because it is a deliberate asymmetry with [P4](#proposal-p4-settling-dataflow-and-narrative-heat):** **causality propagates; licence does not.** Permission has no conservation law and no downstream obligation --- a licence is held, not transmitted. As a [relation-kind register](#the-relation-kind-register) entry, licence's propagation policy is **none**, which no other kind in the register has.

**Re-open trigger, set now while it costs nothing --- and set at *consequence*, not at visibility.** ~~A player-visible contradiction.~~ **Raised 2026-08-09, the same day, and the correction matters:** player-visible is the **minimum** bar, not the bar. Per [Fiction is a fault-tolerant medium](#fiction-is-a-fault-tolerant-medium--and-what-that-does-not-excuse-2026-08-09), a contradiction a player merely *notices* is inside the tolerance --- audiences do repair work, and the alternatives to accepting that are all worse. **The trigger is a contradiction a player *acted on*** --- reasoning from an absence and being contradicted by the world is agency undermined --- **or one that breaks a stake the fiction had established.** Also unconditionally: **any *structural* defect** (two napkins where there is one, a mis-keyed referent) is a bug regardless, never covered by this decision, and never a reason to re-open it either. **And weight the trigger by salience** --- a contradiction the fiction never leaned on is cheap even when noticed; one that contradicts something a scene was *about* is not.

### Open questions (P5 sub-questions; ids stable, never reused)

**Parallel to P3's PQ series and P4's PS series.**

| ID | Question |
| --- | --- |
| **PL-1** | **Of all the positive and negative facts held by containing abstractions higher in the fractal, how do we reasonably determine which are *relevant* to improvisation at a local level?** **The central open question of this proposal**, and the one [C20](#recommended-order) exists to pressure. **Note three things about the framing, each load-bearing.** **(i) It spans positive facts as well as negative ones**, so it is wider than licence: a tavern authored with a roaring fire should inform local improvisation (soot, shadows, warmth) without that fact being a permission at all. **P5's licence frame covers only the negative channel**, and this question is the reason not to mistake the proposal for the whole mechanism. **(ii) *Relevant*, deliberately, and not *salient*** --- [salience is already spoken for](#narrative-integrity-is-in-service-to-reincorporation-2026-08-09), where it measures how much contradicting a fact would strain, and the two come apart (a player's *my character hates cats* is strain-expensive and irrelevant to a place setting; the tavern's napkin stock is the reverse). **Relevance is also the word [PS-4](#open-questions-p4-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused) already uses for context selection, which is correct rather than coincidental --- see (iii).** **(iii) This is the initiative's founding question arriving inside P5.** *Which ancestor facts belong in this packet* is [C3](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c3-the-snare-trap-coyote-cannot-name)'s scoping problem, AB-22's affinity filter and AB-24's ambient tier, one level in. **Reassuring** --- no new hard problem is being invented --- **and cautionary**, since P5 thereby inherits an unsolved one. **Candidate answers, listed to keep the space open rather than to rank them:** copy-at-import ([C19](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c19-filling-out-the-place-setting-and-the-tavern-that-ran-out-of-napkins)'s shipped-in-design mechanism, and **partial by construction** --- it says nothing about entities that never imported, which is clause 10's gap); kind-based (some fact kinds containment-transitive, others local); weight-based (**AB-46**); depth- or distance-based; semantic proximity (the objects lane already carries embeddings); LLM judgment at mint time; explicit authorial declaration of reach. **The illustration to reason from, inherited 2026-08-09 from the retired C20 and sharper here than it was there.** A tavern authored **poor** containing a place setting: poverty should plausibly reach inward and stop silver flatware being improvised. A tavern **out of napkins**: [C19](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c19-filling-out-the-place-setting-and-the-tavern-that-ran-out-of-napkins) beat 5 says it need **not** reach the same place setting. **Structurally identical, and the discriminator is therefore not structural.** **What settles it is a causal story, not a rule:** *perhaps the tavern is out of napkins precisely because the last one was laid on that place setting* --- under which the napkin exists and explains the shortage. **And the opposite answer is equally fine:** judge no-napkins relevant and the setting simply lacks one. **Both produce coherent fiction, which is the strongest evidence that relevance is *reasoning* rather than retrieval** --- and therefore that the answer to this row is an [AB-19](#open-decisions-design--plan-only)-style bounded LLM step over a well-chosen packet, not a lookup table. **Expecting a rule to adjudicate poverty-versus-napkins was the wrong shape of expectation**, and it cost a falsification case to notice.

**One distinction this row now owns, promoted from P5 clause 10:** **permission is *copied* at import; context is *read live*.** That is what keeps clause 6's no-cascade from conflicting with live relevance, and it is the difference between *imported-from* and *merely-inside*.

**One check already available against the kind-based candidate:** a fixed table of which fact kinds propagate is close to the compiled-vocabulary move **AB-19 rejected**, so it needs a fallback rather than an enumeration --- which is a real constraint on it, not a disqualification |

### The cost that is not optional, and it is AB-19's

**If licence is only ever authored, P5 recreates the thing AB-19 rejected.** That row killed the authored-pattern-vocabulary shortcut partly on the objection that it *"limits players to what someone anticipated"* --- and a licence system whose limits all trace to an author is that objection with a new name. **So mechanisms by which licence widens in play are load-bearing for P5's acceptability, not a later refinement.** One is already available and should not be mistaken for the whole answer: **licence governs what the *system* may invent, not what the world may become.** A player who puts a napkin on a closed place setting is not improvising --- the whole gains a member by ordinary mutation, and the licence was never in the path.

## Parked extension points this initiative would unpark

**Framing (2026-08-05, from the author of most of these):** these were laid down deliberately, in anticipation of a wider-reaching reasoning effort that would eventually need them. **This is that effort.** So unparking is the *expected shape* of implementation here, not a cost to be minimized or a sign the slice is too big --- and a candidate slice that unparks nothing is more likely mis-aimed than cheap.

Every row below was verified in this repo during the design conversation. Each is a **deliberate** deferral with a stated trigger, not an oversight.

| Parked capability | Where it is recorded | What unparks it |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **`OBJECT#` / Area membership hosts** | [`positions/AGENT.concepts.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.concepts.md#fractal-position-graphs-container-scale-and-edges) --- "deferred until needed" | Composition, if part-of resolves as a host relationship (AB-4). Nesting is the case that makes them needed. |
| **A root concept on `EphemeraPositionGraph`** | [`positionGraph/AGENT.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/positionGraph/AGENT.md#known-limitation-deferred) --- pinned in two places, with `CarryClosureFragment` set to collapse into it | AB-3. The reciprocal note exists precisely so whoever adds a root here finds it. |
| **Features as graph nodes** | Same --- node `tag` union is `'Character' \| 'Object'` only | AB-12's Room/Feature half. Note Features are designed as *narrower* participants, not Object peers. |
| **Character-relation widening (BD-36)** | Same --- `HostRelationalEdge` endpoints are object-only; `removeCharacter`'s assert is vacuously satisfied today | Relations over non-object nodes; the assert is the placeholder already installed for that day. |
| **Reverse edge-reference index / post-clear sweep** ***(most load-bearing --- three independent consumers)*** | Same --- adjacency indexes *node* placement, not edge-only references | Cross-host abstraction membership (AB-6, AB-8); part-connections referencing a licensing relation (AB-26); and the backwards discovery a licence-removing mutation needs (AB-29). Three separate lines of reasoning arrived here independently on 2026-08-05. |
| **Render-cache `n` / mark-state axis + `situationId`** | [`renderCache/AGENT.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/renderCache/AGENT.md); pinned to `[]` and `SITUATION#DEFAULT` by iteration 10 | AB-23. Situational *meaning* is this axis raised from rendering to interpretation. |
| **`thinking` `claimed` status + a claimer** | [`thinking/AGENT.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/thinking/AGENT.md); status shipped, no producer, retention already protects queued work | AB-18, AB-24 --- deferred recognition and the ambient context tier. |
| **`EMBEDDING#PERSPECTIVE#...` rows** | [`objects/AGENT.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/objects/AGENT.md) Follow-ups --- "when perspective-scoped similarity is needed" | AB-23. Perspective-scoped semantic similarity *is* situational meaning, expressed in the embedding layer. |
| **WML `Relational` tag / `EdgeList` consolidation** | [`positions/AGENT.concepts.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.concepts.md#wml-convergence-future) --- deferred pending heterogeneous room/container edge lists | AB-10, AB-15 --- authored composition patterns and authored interpretive frames. |

| **Backtrack channel (BD-18)** | [`AGENT.backtrackChannel.planning.md`](../actions/AGENT.backtrackChannel.planning.md) --- iteration 4, named not started | AB-20: an LLM that proposes a wrong recognition needs a structured critique path, not a silent guess. |
| **Moving an object into or out of another object's interior graph** | Nowhere yet --- newly named 2026-08-07. `MutationKernelTransferStep`'s singular `toHostId` and `applyStepSequenceCore` were built against ordinary hosts (rooms, characters); neither has been asked whether it can source from or target a node nested inside a whole's own `positionGraph` | **AB-40(a)**, from [C13](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c13-taking-and-returning-the-fork). **Recognized, not designed:** this is not an open fork between candidate answers, it is a scoped capability H3 requires and today's kernel has never been asked to supply --- the ordinary "which of these is actually a decision" test this table's framing note applies. |

**Use this table in Phase 5.** A slice's cost is substantially "which of these does it unpark, and is that one ready to be unparked" --- several have their own prerequisites, and at least one (the backtrack channel) is a sibling initiative that would need scheduling rather than absorbing.

### Shipped code this initiative would retire

**The mirror image of the table above --- not a capability to unpark, but one to decommission.** Named here so Phase 6 (graduate the slice) doesn't rediscover it from scratch.

| Shipped mechanism | Why it's a retirement candidate | What replaces it |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Carry closure** --- `computeCarryClosure`, `classifyInteractionUnderTransfer`, `CarryClosureFragment` ([`interactionUnderTransfer.ts`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/positionGraph/expandValidate/interactionUnderTransfer.ts)) | Opened 2026-08-07 from [C14](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c14-the-tray-its-cups-and-how-many-times-it-gets-picked-up). Two sub-mechanisms, both independently found to already have H3-native equivalents: the BFS-walk-and-bundle half collapses into ephemeral-whole construction (**AB-41**); the boundary-edge classification half already reuses closely-related vocabulary (`dissolve`/`carry`/`defer` --- three outcomes, not the four this plan has been citing; see AB-28) to what ports' own boundary-edge discipline uses (AB-28's fifth independent arrival). **Not a parallel path that resembles the abstraction framework --- it is the abstraction framework, called before the framework existed.** | Ephemeral-abstraction construction (AB-41) for the bundling half; `boundaryEdgeOutcomes` directly for the classification half, once H3's whole/port machinery is what a transfer operation is built against |

**Do not act on this before H3 ships.** Carry closure is today's only working mechanism for this problem; retiring it is a Phase 6 consequence, not a prerequisite for anything earlier in this plan.

## Phase 0 corpus

**Moved 2026-08-07 to [`AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md`](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md).** The corpus grew to ten cases and ~270 lines, and it is the block most often read on its own and least often edited --- so it lives beside this plan rather than inside it, per [`taskPlanning/AGENT.md`](../../../../AGENT.md)'s guidance on keeping bulk out of a plan body. **It is not archived and not optional reading:** Phases 1 and 2 argue from it directly, and its per-case annotation tallies are their input.

Cases to date: C1 (Room/Feature participation), C2 (the rope refused at the gate), C3 (the snare Coyote cannot name), C4 (the Faerie Court place setting), C5 (the chain mounted at both ends), C6 (the impromptu timer), C7 (Ariadne's thread), C8 (the flashlight that vanished), C9 (coiling the rope back in), C10 (the moonbase computer system), C11 (the Rube Goldberg machine), C12 (the moonbase power system and the cut cable), C13 (taking and returning the fork). Remaining cases are listed under [Phase 0](#recommended-order).

## Open decisions (design --- plan only)

Plan-only: decisions being made in order to design this initiative. When one ships, record it in the relevant durable doc ([`positions/AGENT.contract.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.contract.md) for normative rules, [`AGENT.concepts.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.concepts.md) for graduated vocabulary) and remove the row here.

**IDs are stable and never reused**, following the ladder's BD-N convention.

**Requirement rows and representation rows are different, and most rows here are the second kind.** Noticed 2026-08-06 when AB-4 turned out to be conflating both. *What must the model be able to express?* is answerable from the fiction with confidence. *What is the representation?* is not, and depends on the first. A row asking both makes the answerable half unreachable --- so when a row resists, **check whether it is really two rows** before treating it as hard. AB-4 / AB-32 is the worked example; AB-1 is the main pre-existing requirements row. **Splitting a conflated row is not the same as adding scope:** the count rises, the surface does not.

**Rows whose answer is *derived from* a working hypothesis carry an `H1-dependent` marker in their Status cell**, per the tagging test in [Working hypotheses](#working-hypotheses). Merely being *compatible* with H1 earns no marker. The set of markers is the rollback unit if H1 is withdrawn, and its size is a deliberate measurement --- do not apply it defensively.

| ID | Question | Constrained by | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **AB-0** | **Is "Abstraction" the right name, and is it one name or several?** ~~Provisional working term only.~~ **Resolved 2026-08-07: the name is *Abstraction Fractal*, locked.** The Improvisation mechanism/trigger collision that this row also carried is now **AB-38** --- **split out rather than left inside a resolved row**, since a live sub-question buried in a closed row is the dangling-pointer anti-pattern [`taskPlanning/AGENT.md`](../../../../AGENT.md) names. **What settled it, and it is not "we achieved scale-invariance."** The name's central commitment --- *self-similar across scale, no privileged level* --- stopped being prose and became two corpus findings: [C6](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c6-the-rope-the-candle-and-the-impromptu-timer) gives **not a tree** (one part, two wholes) and [C11](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c11-the-rube-goldberg-machine) gives **no privileged level** (a four-level chain, every middle term both part and whole). Those are the two properties that make *fractal* operational rather than evocative, and neither was evidence when the term was proposed. C11 also strengthens the name's payload from the other side: if whole and part are **roles**, there is nothing distinctive at any level to type --- which is the "no `Abstraction` type" position the name exists to argue. **The second reason is about this plan's own failure mode.** The term is already load-bearing throughout these three files. *Marked provisional, acted on as settled* is exactly the shape of "hold even 1a loosely" --- a hedge that sat for days and was then treated as a theorem. **A caveat nothing acts on becomes an assumption**, so locking the name with its limits attached is strictly safer than leaving a provisional label on a term everything already leans on. **Locked with its negative statement**, on the port scheme's precedent: **there is no `AbstractionFractal` type, entity, or record.** The name is the organizing principle. This is not pedantry --- the name reads like a noun while asserting that there is nothing to type, so someone will mint the type, and minting it falsifies the claim. **Three known departures, recorded with the name so the metaphor cannot paper over them:** (1) **DAG, not a tree** (AB-13) --- permanent conceptual limit; (2) **finite depth with a base case** --- a real fractal recurses infinitely and this one bottoms out, with **PQ-13** deciding whether the bottom is uniform; (3) **aspirational above the room** --- unchanged by anything since 2026-08-06, since Room/Area containment is still a structure of different provenance. **Departure 3 carries a visibility hazard rather than only a cost:** the lock already logged that *the room/area seam is now less visible, not less real*, and **a name asserting uniformity makes it harder still to see.** Adopting the name raises that risk; stating it loudly at the point of adoption is the mitigation, not a reason to decline. **What would un-name it: AB-1 resolving as three genuinely unlike things.** The name assumes one substrate. The dependency has moved **favourably** --- C11's roles-not-kinds pushes AB-1 toward *one substrate, distinct types above it* --- but AB-1 is formally open, so the name is **falsifiable by AB-1 rather than waiting on it.** **Discharged 2026-08-07: AB-1 resolved as one substrate**, which is the answer the name assumed --- so the name's largest outstanding risk is closed one day after it was locked. The remaining departures are unaffected. **Graduated to [`AGENT.concepts.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.concepts.md#abstraction-fractal)** with the negative statement and all three departures in full, not as a pointer: a durable doc that adopts a metaphor without its limits is how the metaphor becomes the argument. | AB-1, AB-12, AB-38 | **Locked 2026-08-07** --- earned below the room, aspirational above it, falsifiable by AB-1 |
| **AB-1** | **One concept or several?** Do composition, functional-state aggregate, and multi-host extent share a structure (one type with facets), share only a membership mechanism (a common substrate, distinct types above it), or share nothing but a motivation? **Root question --- every other row reads differently by its answer.** **Resolved 2026-08-07: option 2 --- one substrate, distinct relation kinds above it**, covering all three facets. **The evidence is of two different kinds and they should not be blurred.** *Structural, from the corpus:* [C6](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c6-the-rope-the-candle-and-the-impromptu-timer)'s impromptu timer and [C11](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c11-the-rube-goldberg-machine)'s machine are functional aggregates that turned out to be **ordinary compositions** --- a whole with members, needing no separate mechanism --- in cases built for other purposes. *Requirements, from [C12](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c12-the-moonbase-power-system-and-the-cut-cable):* every construct on the positional side has a **state twin** (roles not kinds, on-demand improvisation, coarsening, the summarization seam, scale-relative truth, no privileged level), so a coarse causal rule and its exceptions are the same graph-connectivity problem this initiative has been solving. **The requirements argument is the same class as AB-32's same-mechanism requirement**, which has been decisive here twice. **Emphatically not option 1.** Two disanalogies: *powers* is **directed** where part-of is containment, so traversal rules differ; and the door is **attached to** the power system without being **part of** it, so a member set is participants rather than parts. Both argue for the middle option, and the substrate already tolerates them --- AB-26's licensing rule is *any kind, any provenance*. **"Unify AB-1" without this clause is ambiguous in the direction that costs more later.** **One cost accepted, and it is the real blast radius.** Unification pulls **AB-6 toward *derived*** --- a causal answer is reachability through intact edges and cannot be cached, since a cut anywhere invalidates it --- while position pulls AB-6 toward **stored**, because `computeStepSequenceFootprint` runs once. Two good reasons pulling opposite ways. **Softening:** state removes the option of dodging a question rather than adding one, since C7's derived-presence walk is the same computation. Evaluation timing is now **AB-39**. **One bound, held by charter rather than by argument.** Reachability is binary; magnitudes that combine along a graph (pressure, temperature) would need arithmetic position never needs. The system hands a reasoning step *"powered; one fault, on the airlock branch"* and lets it infer. That is the seam, not a solver. **Two debts this resolution created, both noted 2026-08-07 rather than left to be rediscovered.** (i) *Distinct relation kinds* names no kinds --- now [the relation-kind register](#the-relation-kind-register), seeded with four and explicitly unstable. (ii) **`positionGraph` will be lying**: causal edges sharing the substrate means the type, the lane name and the graduated concepts vocabulary all describe something narrower than what they hold. Minimum a Phase 6 obligation; whether it needs handling sooner is [open](#the-naming-consequence-positiongraph-will-be-lying). **Note (2026-08-05):** AB-12's candidate reframe would dissolve the *composition* axis into "Object minting at another level," leaving AB-1 to arbitrate only between extent and state. A *derived* member set cannot supply that without already knowing where to look --- the derivation would need the answer it is computing. **What stays open:** the drift-repair story a stored set implies, and whether decay is trim-to-valid (hook 5) or something stronger. **This row is being asked its question twice, possibly with opposite answers --- noticed 2026-08-05 via [C7](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c7-ariadnes-thread).** Part-mediated reference forces **member sets** toward *stored* (the footprint must be known before the walk). Ariadne's thread pulls **presence** --- which rooms a spanning whole occupies --- toward *derived*, since a flat container set cannot supply "the thread now also occupies the room the walker entered" without the same inference either way. Those are different things and the answers may legitimately differ, but the row as written reads as though it has **one** answer. **Split it explicitly in Phase 2** rather than resolving it as a single question and discovering the other half later. **A third answer to the presence half arrived 2026-08-06 from [P3](#ports-are-single-use-and-that-is-not-a-detail-clarified-2026-08-06), in a carrier neither option contemplated: presence stored *in the whole*, as its port list.** Under single-use ports a port's exterior end is a graph plus an optional endpoint, so a presence port is simply one with no endpoint --- and the rope then enumerates the rooms it occupies from its own graph. **That is neither "a stored container set on the part" nor "a predicate derived over room edges"**, which is why the row's two-option framing did not reach it. **Consequence to weigh when splitting: it puts presence and member sets in the *same* carrier**, which is the outcome the double-question worry was written to avoid --- so the split may resolve more cheaply than expected, or may reveal the two answers were never really in tension. **The third carrier stopped being contingent 2026-08-06 when [the frame was locked](#locked-frame-parts-and-ports-2026-08-06):** it arrived via P3 and was therefore only as available as P3 was, but the port list follows from locked clause 2 rather than from any open PQ. **And the presence half acquired a *qualifier* 2026-08-06 from [C10](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c10-the-moonbase-computer-system), which is a different thing from a fourth carrier.** Whatever carries presence, C9's rule for reading it --- at least one port egressing here --- **is falsified as stated**: a moonbase computer system and a rope have identical port topology and require opposite room-scale answers, so presence is **not derivable from the graph at all** and needs a declared property (working name: *apprehension scale*). | **AB-8** | **Multi-host extent.** The rope. **[Proposal P1](#proposal-p1-single-host-objects--hosting-at-the-containing-node) (2026-08-05) proposes resolving this by *removing* multi-host objects entirely** and modelling spanning things as parts with the whole hosted at the containing node --- which would settle this row as framing (c), vindicate the contract clause rather than rewriting it, and move the difficulty into AB-25. Framing (a)'s real costs are therefore that gate, end-state planning collapsing it, a singular `toHostId` on the transfer step, intra-host-only relations, and per-room affordance compose rendering a whole rope in each room --- **five sites on four routes, each of which decided single-host independently.** That multiplicity is itself evidence about the framing: a design the codebase has re-derived against five times is being pushed uphill. **It strengthens P1 symmetrically** --- a single-host design makes the gate correct as written, exactly as it vindicates rather than rewrites the contract clause --- though P1 remains a proposal, not adopted; (b) the abstraction is itself a manipulation host above Room; (c) per-room segment nodes composed by a **composition** abstraction, which would **collapse AB-8 into AB-4**. Note (a) and (c) differ mainly in *where the seam is drawn*, not in capability --- weigh them on which one a player-facing description and a `pull` operation read more naturally from. **Strongest evidence for (c), 2026-08-05 --- [C7, Ariadne's thread](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c7-ariadnes-thread).** Framing (a) represents a spanning thing as **one node** with presence in many hosts, which means there is nowhere to record *where along it* an attachment sits: three attachments on three hosts, a container set that is unordered, and a route that cannot be recovered from exits because a labyrinth has multiple paths. For an object whose **purpose is the route**, (a) is not merely awkward --- it cannot express the object. **This is the clearest discriminator the corpus has produced against (a), and it is about node-versus-parts rather than about hosting**, so it argues for (c) without by itself settling single-host. **Earlier evidence for (c), same day:** a rope *tied to a rock* puts a relation on one end, not on the whole. Under (a) that edge is ambiguous about which host it lives on, and the kernel derives a relational step's host live and throws on mismatch; under (c) it is cleanly cliff-top-segment-to-rock, same host, no new machinery. ~~**Settled sub-point:** extents spanning hosts of *different kinds* (two adventurers holding opposite ends in different rooms) is **not** a concern --- confirmed 2026-08-05.~~ **Reopened the same day, and the reason is instructive: the words did not change but the question did.** That point was settled while framing (a) was live, where it is genuinely a non-issue --- a rope simply has presence in two hosts, of whatever kinds. **Under H1 it becomes a question about the shape of the containment tree**: the whole must host at an LCA of a room and a character, so characters have to be interior nodes of a structure AB-25 frames purely as Room/Area. The row stays Open deliberately: H1 is a hypothesis being tested, not a decision, so (a) and (b) remain live and this row reverts to unframed if H1 is withdrawn.~~ **Reverted 2026-08-07: H1 withdrawn, exactly as this row's own text anticipated.** Framing (c) --- decomposition into parts --- survives as a modeller's *option* under [H3](#h3-ports-as-the-boundary-mechanism-decomposition-as-a-modellers-choice), not as a forced framing; see [C11 Finding 2](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#finding-2-decomposition-is-demoted-from-correctness-to-convenience). (a) and (b) remain live alongside it. Row returns to Open, unframed. |
| **AB-9** | **Which level gets presented, and is that manipulation truth or a projection?** The stated motivation is LLM reasoning scalability, which is a *read-side* problem: the value is in being handed the right level without seeing all levels. Projections are already filters, not the graph (hook 6). **This row most controls total scope** --- if summary is a projection concern, much of axes 1 and 3 never becomes stored truth. Also: is the chosen level perspectival (a novice sees a place setting, an inspector sees five utensils)? **Guardrail, relocated here from AB-14 on 2026-08-05:** description **selects among levels that already exist and must never create one**. If merely looking at something can mint durable structure, then description-time LLM inference becomes a write path, and this row stops controlling scope at all. **Escalated 2026-08-05 by [C5](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c5-the-chain-mounted-at-both-ends), and the change is one of frequency, not kind.** [P1](#proposal-p1-single-host-objects--hosting-at-the-containing-node) already noted it makes multi-level graphs acute; C5 shows co-located parts persist **permanently** whenever they are load-bearing, so a chain mounted at one end and attached to a chest at the other is three nodes in the cell's graph indefinitely. Multi-level is therefore the **steady state of anything anchored**, not a transient during manipulation --- so level selection is a default read-path concern that every describe must answer, which raises this row's weight without changing its question. **The guardrail now has a case that contests it directly --- [C8](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c8-the-flashlight-that-vanished-when-someone-looked-at-it), 2026-08-06.** "Look *carefully* at the flashlight" mints its interior. That is **description acting as a write path**, which this row forbids outright; but AB-14 holds that operational necessity licenses minting and AB-20 that the player's own reference *is* the licence --- and a player asking for detail plausibly qualifies. **Both rows are defensible and they disagree about this case**, which is the first time the guardrail has been contested by anything other than an argument. Candidate resolutions, none chosen: the guardrail holds and close examination is not description but a *command* that happens to read; or the guardrail admits an exception for player-initiated detail requests, which weakens its scope control; or minting is deferred (AB-18's tier 2) so the look itself creates nothing. **Substantially deflated 2026-08-06 by [P2](#proposal-p2-separate-the-storage-shard-from-the-containment-relation) clause 3, though not answered.** "You see a rope, a rope end, and a rope end" is a problem only because parts must be **nodes** to carry relations. With part-addressed edge endpoints the rope is one node and its ends are endpoint qualifiers, so **there is nothing at the membership layer to select between** --- and C5's "permanent multi-level steady state," which is what escalated this row, turns out to have been an artifact of that same constraint rather than a fact about anchored things. **What survives untouched:** the perspectival question (novice versus inspector), and the guardrail collision with AB-14 --- both are about which level to *describe*, which no representation change settles. **Track the deflation honestly: this row was escalated on a premise P2 removes, so re-weigh it before treating it as scope-controlling.** | AB-1, AB-14, AB-20 | Open --- scope-controlling claim needs re-weighing under P2; guardrail contested by C8; **the `H1-dependent` tag on the C6 escalation lapses 2026-08-07 with H1's withdrawal** (the question itself was never H1-dependent, so the P2/C8 tensions stand unaffected) |

The resulting multi-host mutation also has a shipped shape: `MultiKeyUpdate` folds N effects into one `transactWrite`. **So the kernel does not change; a new expansion is written instead.** Notable that the scheme was not designed to answer this row and answers it anyway --- weak evidence the joint is real. **What stays open:** whether *every* abstraction-level operation decomposes this way, or only propagation-shaped ones like `pull`. | AB-1, AB-4, AB-8 | ~~Open --- candidate answer recorded (`H1-dependent`); generality untested~~ **Reverted 2026-08-07: H1 withdrawn.** The Expansion-based answer was argued from H1's whole-hosted-at-Area framing; plausibly re-derivable under [H3](#h3-ports-as-the-boundary-mechanism-decomposition-as-a-modellers-choice) --- a "pull" still needs to reach each port's local part --- but not re-argued here.
