Eager cascade found **structurally impossible** on a once-only footprint rather than merely costly; **retraction dissolved** by reading the model as dataflow rather than truth-maintenance (*the graph holds states, not beliefs*), leaving only topology change, for which *edge deletion emits* --- and the shipped boundary-edge discipline already forbids silent edge disappearance. **PS-1..PS-8 opened.** Not promoted: H1 still holds the hypothesis slot | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **Narrative heat recorded, with the distinction it requires** --- decay is free for **transient/narration** impulses and **lossy for persistent state**, which is a property of the *impulse* not the edge; **heat rising must pull unsettled state to settle** (fourth arrival at the tri-state, first load-bearing one); **decay to *parked*, never to *dead***, since "the trap door did not fire because we rolled badly" is undebuggable --- and the shipped non-TTL retention policy is literally built for parked work. **Heat's affordability rests on having no correctness obligation, which four context consumers would remove** --- tension flagged as **PS-4**, to resolve before building | Done (2026-08-07) |
| ***Parked, never dead* demoted to provisional** --- superseding the confidence of the row above, which asserted it as settled the same day. Two objections: the parked set is an **unbounded history whose worst case lands exactly on a read**, and *never dead* promises **every lead is eventually followed up regardless of whether anyone cared**, which is the wrong discipline for a world to aspire to. **The diagnosis is that the trap-door argument earned *auditable* termination and the section claimed *revivable*** --- different properties, conflated. Three repair directions recorded unadopted (collapse-not-queue, park-only-the-unrederivable, absence-of-heat-as-terminal); **PS-9, PS-10, PS-11 opened** and PS-8 amended, since its escape hatch was the demoted claim | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **CoyoteGame's fitness as prototype recorded, with three seams** --- the genre absorbs the delay artifact *and* (C3) most needs the structure, but a forgiving genre never measures latency, will push to make the beat *reliable* (scheduled delay leaking genre into the substrate), and makes a dropped wave indistinguishable from comic timing. **Both motivating cases are removals of support**, so the algorithmically hard direction is day-one work, not phase-two | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **State declined as a sixth axis** --- fails the frame's own test on audience, function and conflict, and would contradict AB-1 by splitting the evaluation frame where the representation declined to split. **The instinct was catching a missing per-kind fact, not a missing axis** --- lesson recorded generally, since it will recur | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **Relation-kind register opened** --- discharges the debt AB-1 created by committing to "distinct relation kinds" and naming none; seeded with five kinds, only one built; founding finding: **soundness of a mechanism is a property of the relation kind, not of the mechanism**, which coarsening hid for two days by only ever meeting containment. Deliberately **not** graduated --- unstable, and a durable doc listing unbuilt edge kinds is the wishlist anti-pattern | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **`positionGraph` naming consequence recorded** --- causal edges sharing the substrate makes the type, the lane name and the already-graduated concepts vocabulary all narrower than what they hold | Noted (2026-08-07) --- **flagged for its own discussion**; Phase 6 at minimum, possibly sooner |
| **AB-1 resolved --- one substrate, distinct relation kinds above it** --- on two evidence kinds kept separate (structural: C6/C11 functional aggregates turned out to be ordinary compositions; requirements: C12's six-mechanism isomorphism). **Explicitly not one type** --- *powers* is directed and participants are not parts. **Cost accepted: AB-6 pulled toward derived against position's pull toward stored**; bounded by the diegeticLogic charter's no-simulation commitment, which is the stated re-open trigger. AB-0's falsifier discharged; AB-7 narrowed to the carrier and no longer "possibly out of scope"; **AB-39 opened** | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **C12 (moonbase power system, cut cable) written** --- seed example 3, owed since Phase 0 and finally the corpus's first **stateful** aggregate; three carriers checked and all three found wrong (no object-level state, `Meta::Room.state.marks` mutating only by inbound API, no causal edge kind); **coarsening found truth-preserving on containment and truth-destroying on causality**, which upgrades the summarization seam from optimisation to correctness requirement and gives failure-recovery a third needed behaviour (*unknown*); the cut cable shown to be **fission on a causal edge the locked frame already handles** | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **AB-0 split and its naming half locked: *Abstraction Fractal*** --- settled not by achieving scale-invariance but because the commitment became **corpus-grounded** (C6: not a tree; C11: no privileged level) and because a provisional label on a load-bearing term is the "hold 1a loosely" failure waiting to happen; locked **with its negative statement** (no `AbstractionFractal` type --- the name reads like a noun while denying there is anything to type) and **three departures** (DAG not tree; finite depth, base case = PQ-13; aspirational above the room, with the visibility hazard restated); graduated to `AGENT.concepts.md` with the departures **in full**; **AB-38 opened** for the Improvisation mechanism/trigger collision rather than burying it in a resolved row | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **Reasoning axis corrected to *no silent omission*** --- "lossless" overstated within hours of being written; the apprehension/reasoning conflict narrowed (they **agree** on scale and conflict only on member omission) and a candidate resolution recorded; **coarsening found to name two operations** with opposite silence requirements; **ports gain a developed argument on a third axis** (the summarization seam) where both prior arguments were positioning and mutation | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **C7 continued as the corpus's first two-axis case** --- the nesting question (flat spans vs a nested `thread-span` whole) decided on **reasoning alone**, positioning having gone quiet; the snag-ports-through-the-span mechanism identified as structural and the curated summary as question-relative and softer; competitor rescuable only via AB-36, i.e. an epicycle; **AB-37 opened** for the summary's upkeep | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **The five axes framed** --- positioning / mutation / apprehension / reasoning / authoring, with distinct *functions* (filter, veto, rank, rank, rank) rather than five scores; **apprehension-lossy versus reasoning-lossless** named as the conflict that makes it a tool; mutation recognised as cross-cutting rather than part of positioning; coarsening's losslessness claim found **audience-relative and stated unqualified**; falsification criterion restated as *discriminating on a named axis* | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **Corpus retro-fitted to the axes (C1--C11)** --- tally in one place rather than per case; **zero cases primary on authoring**, the largest measured hole; positioning-primary cases confirmed as the ones that discriminated least; C11 found to bite by **internal contradiction**, a mode no axis covers | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **C11 (Rube Goldberg machine) written** --- the corpus's first case with more than two levels, built to challenge an assessment made hours earlier; **clause 1a falsified on a single object** (`StringSpan` is a part and a whole at once, so 1a and multi-host wholes are jointly unsatisfiable) and its *decompose further* justification found to have **no base case**; decomposition demoted from correctness requirement to modelling convenience; **PQ-13 promoted to the fractal's base case**; the `multiPresent`-gate reprieve claimed the same day **withdrawn**, so P1's five-code-sites argument is back in force; *position*-relativity recorded as a second and harder challenge to apprehension scale; locked frame explicitly **not** re-opened, but whole/part clarified as roles | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **Depth named as the corpus's blind spot** --- C1--C10 are all two-level, which is how a disjoint-kinds assumption survived two days unchallenged | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **C20 retired as posed and re-aimed at clause 1; clause 3 reworded; the tavern demoted to a PL-1 illustration.** **The contradiction C20 was queued to exploit was this plan's drafting error, not P5's defect:** clause 3 said a limit reaches *no further* than its holder **without saying in which direction**, and containment scope is directional --- a place setting's limit reaching the tavern is **outside** its holder-scope, the tavern's reaching **inward** is inside it. **No contradiction, and C11's internal-contradiction mode was mis-claimed.** Clause 3 now reads *an entity's scope includes what it contains*, permitting inward reach while leaving **which** facts reach to PL-1: **the clause permits, relevance selects.** **Clause 10 resolved rather than falsified**, and usefully --- **permission is *copied* at import; context is *read live***, which names the missing *imported-from* versus *merely-inside* distinction and stops clause 6's no-cascade conflicting with live relevance. **C20 re-aimed at clause 1** (unbounded by default) via the **tightly-authored closed world**, where an author wants silence to mean *no* --- **independent of relevance**, which the tavern version was not, and whose obvious repair (zero the root, widen below) forces the unworked composition semantics. **The tavern survives as PL-1's illustration**, where it does real work: *poor* and *out of napkins* are structurally identical with different required answers, and what settles them is a **causal story** (*perhaps the tavern is out of napkins because the last one went onto that very place setting*) --- **either answer is coherent fiction, which is the evidence that relevance is reasoning rather than retrieval**, and therefore an AB-19-style bounded LLM step rather than a lookup. **Expecting a rule to adjudicate it was the wrong shape of expectation, and it cost a falsification case to notice.** **Structural finding recorded with it: P5's shape claims stand alone, but P5's *utility* is gated on relevance** --- the initiative's founding unsolved problem --- so P5 can be locked at the shape level and cannot be evaluated for usefulness ahead of PL-1 | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **AB-47 settled --- two constructs, one enforcement point --- after reversing twice in one day.** The one-construct reversal below was itself overturned by an argument neither earlier draft had made: **unify genre boundaries with weighted world facts as *a licence narrowing with weight infinite*, and six consumers each become a branch on whether the weight is infinite** --- mint gate, out-of-character response text, escalation channel, reincorporation weighting, write authority, moderation signal. **Six for six, sharing only a storage shape.** A sentinel that changes behaviour at every read site is **two constructs with extra steps**, and the *one construct with a discriminating field* compromise collapses identically, a field determining every behaviour being a type distinction spelled as an enum. **Stronger than the argument AB-47 opened on**, which depended on someone eventually erring; this one holds under perfect discipline. **The concrete difference is the pair of out-of-character responses:** *persuade other players, outside the fiction* versus *nobody has ever done this --- it is possible, but a long-term quest*. **Draft 2's error recorded because its shape recurs: an objection falling is not a conclusion arriving** (the mint-gate finding removed one of two objections and was treated as making the case) --- the mirror of this plan's standing warning that an unacted-on caveat becomes an assumption. **Two spoils: AB-46 gains semantics** (*weight is the effort required to overturn*, symmetric with investment-behind) **and is bounded away from genre boundaries** (not on the scale at all, so weight is not a universal currency with an infinite value); **and the legibility caution is discharged**, both constructs now having a legible out-of-character response, with the weighted one converting refusal into a **quest hook**. **What survives from the reversal is its durable half: one enforcement point** --- a mint-time gate suffices for both, neither needs a narration-time twin | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **AB-47's lean reversed the day it was opened --- one construct, not two.** Both arguments for a type split fell to one observation: **a mint-time gate is sufficient.** Players cannot be stopped from assembling a tube, powder and shot and asking for a gun; the system can **refuse to mint the abstraction** and thereafter **steadily refuse to reason about it**. **The narration objection was answered by re-scoping, not rebuttal** --- gating structure and tolerating narration drift *is* the integrity->prevention / coherence->damping split, applied where it was already committed; the gate catches the load-bearing minted object and lets a stray NPC line through, which is the correct priority under a **blast-radius** frame rather than a prevention one. **Refusal is structurally silent**: an unminted abstraction produces no packet, so nothing has to obey anything --- **bounded context doing a third job**, after scalability and fault isolation. **The unrepresentability argument shrank from *needs a type* to *needs a field*.** **What survives is write authority** --- social contracts change by out-of-character table negotiation, plausibly a fourth provenance that separates from *authored* only in multiplayer. **Two cautions carried:** silent refusal is safe but not legible (a communication problem, and it cannot be explained in-world), and blast-radius reduction assumes moderators arrive. **Out of scope by agreement:** the table's social contract and moderation practice. **Kept in scope because the mechanism yields it free:** refusal events are already computed, and near-miss licensing makes **approach** detection graded rather than binary --- automated instrumentation of players nearing a genre break would be novel for this class of game, where moderation has been reactive | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **C21 written and AB-47 opened --- the constraint that may not belong in the fiction at all.** *"A fantasy setting in which firearms have not been invented"* and *"a fantasy setting, without firearms"* exclude identically and behave differently on four probes; the decisive one is **defeasibility** --- a player who researches gunpowder should succeed under the first, which locates the constraint **inside** the diegesis, and pushes on the genre boundary under the second, which locates it outside. **Candidate framing: a bare genre-forbid is a *social contract*, not a fictional contribution** --- inviolable rather than weighted (**the first construct in this design that is not a matter of degree**, which bounds AB-46), breached rather than strained, owed no diegetic explanation, and constraining **narration** rather than only world-state. **Unification recorded and not waved off** (a root-level licence with weight infinite absorbs three of four consequences; the narration one does not reduce), with the stronger objection being **type separation makes the category error unrepresentable rather than forbidden** --- C18 Finding 4's move on its **fourth** arrival. **Third instance of the corpus's identical-surface/different-behaviour family** after C3 and C18, and each time the missing thing was *how it came to be that way*. **Scale-invariance checked and passes** (*poor* versus *war-stripped* at tavern scale), so it feeds C20 at no cost. P5 clause 2 shown to flatten the causal form, clause 8 put under pressure (a fact entailing a licence state means **something propagated**), clause 9 given real content | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **PL-1 opened, and C20's predicted repair withdrawn before the case was written.** A queued falsification case carrying a named expected answer gets written **to** that answer, so the prediction (character-limits transitive, inventory-limits local) was struck and **replaced with the question**: *of all the positive and negative facts held by containing abstractions higher in the fractal, how do we reasonably determine which are **relevant** to improvisation at a local level?* **Three properties of that framing are load-bearing.** It spans **positive** facts, so it does not presuppose licence is the only channel ancestor context travels --- **which means P5's frame covers one channel of two**. It says **relevant**, not *salient*, avoiding a collision with the strain measure (the two come apart in both directions) and correctly matching the word PS-4 already uses. And it is **the initiative's founding question --- C3/AB-22/AB-24's context-scoping problem --- arriving one level in**, which is reassuring (no new hard problem) and cautionary (P5 inherits an unsolved one). Seven candidates listed unranked, with copy-at-import noted as **partial by construction**; the withdrawn prediction gains a strike it did not have when made, since a fixed propagation table is close to the compiled-vocabulary move **AB-19 rejected**. Licence composition folded in as PL-1's licence-shaped half | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **P5 decomposed into 11 numbered clauses, and C20 queued as its first falsification case.** Numbered **before** the case rather than after, on H3's precedent, so a failure lands on a clause rather than on the proposal. **The exercise paid immediately: four claims P5 was already relying on had never been stated** --- clause **9** (a licence is a *shaped space*, not a scalar --- *warm but poor* cannot ride a single dial), clause **10** (entities not related by import have no licence relationship, which leaves an authored place setting unrelated to its container), clause **11** (authoring sets licence unattenuated), and a correction to clause **7**: C18 stated the discriminator as *intensional -> open*, but a **poor** tavern is intensional and **narrow**, so intensionality settles whether a licence exists and what **shape** it has, not whether it is open. **An unstated claim cannot be falsified**, so all four were untestable until numbering. **Clause 3 recorded as under direct attack** --- C20 requires it to both not-reach outward and reach inward, C11's internal-contradiction mode, and sharpest beside C19 beat 4 where the same structure demands the opposite answer. **P5's status decision is explicitly blocked on C20**, rather than promoted on the strength of three supporting cases | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **Narrative integrity redefined as service to *reincorporation*, and AB-46 opened.** In a story game, integrity protects **participant contributions being built on** rather than internal consistency as such --- so a tavern flipping well-stocked to poor is near-costless when the system improvised both, and expensive when an author wrote it or players **worked in play** to stock it. **Supersedes salience as the criterion and demotes it to a proxy** (*salience measures attention; contribution measures investment*), and **replaces the licence-clustering heuristic with an exact structural signal that already exists**: the authored / player-driven / system-improvised split in storage. **Third dent in AB-14's "provenance is invisible downstream," and restated rather than patched** --- invisible in the *merged view*, fully recoverable from *storage*; only the first was ever argued. **Division of labour clarified:** reincorporation covers authored and player commitments, tone, genre and stakes; structural well-formedness covers the reality illusion; neither covers the ground alone. **First load-bearing frame in this plan taken from theatre craft** (Johnstone's reincorporation) rather than from computing. **AB-46 records narrative weight** --- carried *on* the record as a value, not a pointer, so it composes with P5's no-cascade rather than reintroducing the provenance graph that decision declined | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **"Salience is not structural" withdrawn the same day it was written** --- challenged on the grounds that **a scene about the tavern's poverty goes hand in hand with minting licence restrictions**, so the salient and incidental cases *do* differ structurally: poverty **is** a closed licence, while the incidental case has no record at all. **Salience leaves traces, and the primary trace is licence being touched.** Recorded as a **correlate, not an identity**, with counterexamples in both directions (a scene about generosity is salient and may only *widen* licence, or touch none; C19 beat 4 narrows licence and matters to nobody). Three candidate signals named, none assessed --- licence touched at all, **clustering** of related changes (the most promising and cheapest), heat at establishment. **Makes the damping rule cheaper to act on**, since *where the story has invested* becomes partly readable. **One speculative consequence flagged for testing:** if a place's character is largely the shape of its licence, then **licence is the generative half of setting character**, which is a far larger claim than P5 currently makes | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **"Fault" redefined for the medium, and the terminology collision it exposed fixed.** A fault is **not** *the wrong fiction* --- **there is no wrong fiction** --- it is **something that strains the integrity of the story.** Two consequences: **accuracy is the wrong standard**, so validating against truth is the wrong machinery (what is checkable is consistency with what the fiction already committed to, a far smaller obligation); and **strain tracks salience**, so the damping rule sharpens from *damp faults* to **damp faults in proportion to salience** --- global consistency was never the target. **A third job for narrative heat**, fed to PS-4 with that row's own warning attached rather than treated as answered. **Terminology fixed rather than absorbed:** *integrity* was doing two jobs, now split into **narrative integrity** (the graded measure), **structural well-formedness** (non-negotiable), and **coherence** (one component of the first, not a synonym). **Structural defects re-justified under the single measure** as *latent strain* --- they cash out later as the world failing to behave like a world --- rather than as a category exempt from it. **Negative statement extended:** *no wrong fiction* **relocates** the standard rather than removing it, since it is quotable in exactly the way the section title is | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **The feedback regime named as the thing to measure, and as a design principle for the system generally.** Fault tolerance under LLM-in-the-loop has two regimes --- **damping** (faults corrected or papered over as the story proceeds; fine) and **amplifying** (faults spawning larger faults; problematic) --- and **the rule adopted is to seek solutions promoting the damping regime rather than solutions that leave amplification in place and try to prevent error entirely.** Mapped onto the existing line: **integrity -> prevention** (keep the gates and asserts), **coherence -> damping**. Four dampers identified, two of them **new arguments for existing mechanisms** (coarsening as a low-pass filter, so the port gains a *fourth* argument on a fourth axis; and bounded context as fault isolation, making the scalability and stability theses one mechanism). Three amplifiers named, **the first being the Abstraction Fractal's own no-privileged-level commitment** --- arbitrary-depth summaries-of-summaries --- with P4 cycles and licence widened on improvised grounds after it. **Declined as a sixth axis** on the grounds that it is a different *kind* of question (dynamics of a design, not a value the graph serves). **P4 iteration 1 gains a fourth instrumented output: fault propagation**, via contradiction frequency, summarization-chain depth, and whether contradiction rises with world age. Flagged as a **graduation candidate**, held back because nothing is built | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **Fiction recorded as a fault-tolerant medium, as a standing frame** with three existing consumers (coarsening, PS-5's glitch policy, P5's no-cascade) rather than a clause inside any one of them. **Some player-visible contradiction is fine** --- a property of the medium, not a concession, and every alternative to accepting it (refuse to improvise / trace everything / block on settling) costs more. **The useful half is the line: integrity versus coherence** --- structural well-formedness is non-negotiable, story consistency is negotiable, and P5 spends only the second. **Recorded with its negative statement on AB-0's precedent**, since the phrase will be quoted to excuse defects: it does not cover integrity failures, does not cover a contradiction a player **acted on** (agency undermined, not flavour), and **does not obviously extend to LLM context**, whose tolerance is a second and unmeasured thing. **General rule adopted: set re-open triggers at *consequence*, not detectability** --- and P5's trigger corrected accordingly the same day it was written | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **C19 written and licence transfer settled --- import is a copy, provenance is not traced.** A recognized (licence-zero) place setting is *filled out* on player instruction, **importing** licence from the tavern and becoming independent; a later tavern-level narrowing (no napkins in stock) **does not reach it**. **The import needs no new mechanism** --- it is the whole acquiring the **intensional description** C18 already identified as the discriminator, which also shows C18's three cases are **states, not kinds**, and that the negative lock is not permanent. **Cascade explicitly declined**, with the note that the local answer is also the *fictionally* correct one here (the napkin was laid before the storeroom was found empty), so this is a preference rather than a scalability concession. Mis-scoped limits repaired by **scope, not propagation** --- the scope move's third arrival. **General result: causality propagates, licence does not** --- the register's first kind with propagation policy *none*. Re-open trigger set at **player-visible** contradiction, explicitly not at theoretical incoherence | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **P5 recorded --- improvisational licence as a first-class graph item**, from conversation, reorganizing the negatives cluster the same day it was opened. **Licence is unbounded by default; a negative is a recorded *narrowing* of it; a blanket negative is a narrowing to zero.** Four specific repairs: C18's scope trap becomes **unrepresentable rather than forbidden** (a rule turned into an invariant), AB-43's blanket half **dissolves** (keyed by its holder --- the original question was a category error), AB-42's **accumulation cost collapses** to one coarsenable licence per entity, and AB-45's **provenance collision closes** (licence is carried by the whole; provenance only sets its initial value, so AB-14's merge-layer claim survives). Consistency check run and passed: *not established* still costs nothing. **AB-44 survives narrower and better-defined** --- licence-closure universal, causal force variable, with authoring the exception preventing collapse. **One cost named as non-optional:** if licence is only ever authored, P5 recreates AB-19's *limits players to what someone anticipated* objection, so widening-in-play mechanisms are load-bearing rather than a later refinement | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **C18 (three place settings) written, and AB-45 opened** --- the axis's second case, one day after C17 opened it. Three structurally **identical** `PlaceSetting` wholes (authored / minted from a coarse envelope / recognized over five existing objects) carry **different improvisation licences**, so licence is **not readable off the graph**. Discriminator identified as **intensional versus extensional** rather than provenance, with improvisation **gap 2's own sentence** ("its body is nearly empty and its member set is the content") found to state the rule it was written to describe as a shortfall. **C17 Finding 6's guardrail restatement confirmed** on a case it was not derived from (the C8 test still owed). **The completeness trap named:** a licence record must deny the whole's *generative capacity*, never assert *there is nothing else here*, which would re-commit C17's closed-world defect one scale down. Shown to fall outside AB-42--AB-44's scope on all three counts, giving a **second independent route** to AB-44's per-kind answer; **AB-14's provenance-invisibility claim dented a second time** and flagged for settling with C17 Finding 7 | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **AB-42, AB-43, AB-44 opened --- the negatives cluster**, from C17 Finding 7 and the causal-absence argument (*a flashlight gives no light, deducible from no battery, without knowing its other details*). **AB-42** (node or collection) carries P2's phantom-edge argument applied one scale down --- a collection of facts that cannot be edge endpoints is the shape P2 exists to remove --- against two unpriced costs, accumulation and node-kind. **AB-43** (what a negative is keyed by) recorded as an explicit **non**-dependency of AB-42, since a cost both horns share cannot discriminate. **AB-44** (do all negatives carry causal force) placed **upstream** of AB-42, as AB-19's description/mechanism split arriving a third time, with lifecycle/decay parked inside it rather than given a fourth row. Constraint matrix updated, and the diagram's AB-1--AB-24 scope stated so it is not read as complete | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **C17 (the professional kitchen) written --- the corpus's first authoring-primary case**, closing the tally's largest measured hole after sixteen cases and three that passed it by. Establishes a **three-valued detail space** --- established present / established absent / **not established** --- in which the third value is **settled rather than pending** and is represented by **nothing at all**, so the storage gap is exactly one item: the negative record. `noMatch` identified as the **closed-world assumption** answering *established absent* to every unasked question. **A naming rule adopted with it:** the detail space is infinite, so *unsettled / undetermined / pending / partial / incomplete / remainder / exhaustive* are all defects --- enumerated for rejection, on AB-0's lock-the-name-with-its-negative-statement precedent. Also: a **fourth improvisation gap** (no artifact for a negative answer), a **fifth PS-3 consumer with the opposite read obligation**, a **fourth outcome** for the clean/dirty table, and a **third branch for AB-29** (asset-*added*), where the participation stack is shown unable to arbitrate because existence is not merge output | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **Withdraw H1 and state its successor** --- no clause of 1 survives C11; successor must predicate hosting on something uniform at every level, not on whole-versus-part | **Next** --- authorized in principle, deferred until the decomposition-as-convenience thread ([C11 finding 2](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#finding-2-decomposition-is-demoted-from-correctness-to-convenience)) is worked, since it may change what the successor claims |
| **Work through the remaining PQ questions** --- P3's open sub-questions | In progress --- PQ-1 settled; **PQ-11 (ingress drift repair) is the newest open item**; PQ-6/PQ-7 deflated by the single-use design; PQ-10 is a bounded matcher fix; PQ-4/PQ-8/PQ-9 still carry leanings with alternatives not yet on the table |
| **Clause 1b: decide whether H1 is withdrawn** | Not started --- pressure tallied from four directions, rollback set measured at six rows; 1a and clause 3 survive either way, so the live question is 1b + entailments. **Re-framed 2026-08-06 by [C10](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c10-the-moonbase-computer-system) before being decided: the (a)/(b) hosting fork may never have been one global question.** Not a fifth pressure --- it questions the shape of the choice rather than pushing a side. Read C10 first |
| AB-36 --- reverse edge-reference index; blocks P3 and carries a shipped guarantee (PQ-7) | Not started --- four consumers identified, argument recorded so it is not re-derived |
| **AB-32 --- collect the composition requirements; blocks AB-4, which blocks clause 4/5 testing** | In progress --- three requirements answered (wholes nest; same reference mechanism; relations attach to parts without part-nodes); **its decider needs re-testing under P2** |
| AB-33 --- does the host link become a real labeled edge, and the host a node? | Not started --- keep the cheap reading (node in its own graph) separate from the expensive one (global edge space) |
| AB-35 --- `positionCache` derivation, invalidation, and the never-a-second-truth rule | Not started --- invariant proposed, sub-questions open |
| Containment/extent separability --- can one composition be both (ship's rigging)? | **Answered 2026-08-06 by the wagon-in-a-doorway case: yes, and routinely.** Spanning and containment are independent axes. No longer a premise anything rests on |

## Lifecycle

**Three files dispose together** --- this plan, [`AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md`](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md), and [`AGENT.abstractionLayers.discussion.planning.md`](AGENT.abstractionLayers.discussion.planning.md). The split is an organizational convenience, not three lifecycles. **One asymmetry to decide at disposal rather than now:** the corpus is the only part with a plausible claim to outliving the initiative --- worked examples that a later reader could use to test a *further* change --- so when this plan is retired, ask whether the corpus graduates somewhere durable instead of being deleted with the rest.

Dispose once the design settles and its lasting content lives in [`positions/AGENT.concepts.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.concepts.md) / [`AGENT.contract.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.contract.md) / [`AGENT.implementation.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.implementation.md) --- **unless** Phase 7 concludes this is a multi-iteration ladder, in which case this file converts to a standing index (same posture as [`AGENT.objectManipulationIterations.planning.md`](../actions/AGENT.objectManipulationIterations.planning.md)) and its lifecycle note is rewritten accordingly.
